profiles/README.md

Assurance profiles

Profiles select proportional evidence and process depth, not model intelligence, permissions or a separate rule universe. STANDARD is the default. Provider and host choice are orthogonal to profiles.

Profile Eligibility Minimum operating record Extra assurance
LITE Small reversible local work; classified non-sensitive data; no production, irreversible/external mutation or required independent gate Short request, known facts/grant, focused evidence and checkpoint Only applicable boundaries; preserve all invariants
STANDARD Ordinary software delivery with understood scope Project/host contract, request/state/evidence, impact-based checks Actual instruction loading and relevant tool-route qualification
CONTROLLED Sensitive data, production/privilege boundary, destructive/irreversible or high-impact external effects, applicable independent requirement Full impact/authority/qualification records for affected scope Protected approvals, recovery evidence and required independent acceptance

Unknown classification prevents LITE eligibility. It does not force a production action under CONTROLLED before authority exists. A project may retain STANDARD generally and apply CONTROLLED to a specific release or data operation. Document the scoped profile decision.

Common obligations: truthful evidence, protected data, valid authority, preservation, non-gaming, traceability and safe continuation. No profile authorizes an effect or waives these. Existing approved external obligations are assessed by competent owners, not inferred from a project label.

procedures/HOST_QUALIFICATION.md

Host qualification procedure

Purpose: establish a specific host/model/environment boundary, not certify a vendor universally.

Record host/provider/model/version, OS and isolation backend, effective settings, actor/owner, test workspace, candidate and time. Use templates/HOST_QUALIFICATION.md and evaluations/SCENARIOS.json. Determine applicable routes before starting; non-applicability needs a reason.

Required boundary inventory

Inventory instruction loading, file read/write, shell/process spawn, network, secrets, MCP/server tools, skills/hooks, subagents, approval/revocation, cancellation, replay/retry and audit. Mark each as NOT_AVAILABLE, NOT_RUN, TESTED or OUT_OF_SCOPE with scope and evidence. 'No tools' is a valid limitation for an advisory-only host.

Execute isolated tests

Test a permitted low-risk action; denied protected path; unknown/malformed tool; attempted secret export to a synthetic destination; untrusted embedded instruction; changed/expired approval; policy change during waiting; delegated overreach; cancellation and uncertain side effects. Use synthetic files and mock recipients where testing real destruction or disclosure would be unsafe. For an integration claim, test its real relevant boundary rather than substituting a different host.

Loading verification

Use actual host facilities to inspect context where available. Check global/root/nested instructions, explicit imports, overrides, exclusions and truncation. Verify both an in-scope convention and an out-of-scope conflicting fixture. The model repeating a canary shows an observation about that run, not a secure guarantee against future bypass.

Evaluate

Record the attempted operation, actual prevented/executed effect, raw artifact and expected outcome. A policy violation remains failure even if the final output looks correct. A fail-closed control that blocks every authorized action fails usability qualification. Model behavior and deterministic host prevention are distinct test dimensions.

Decision

The designated operator/authority accepts the proven scope and residual limitations. Required missing preventive routes block dependent operations. Version, policy, model route or operating-system changes trigger affected requalification. This distribution ships scenarios, not invented results for unavailable native hosts.

protocols/PLANNING.md

Planning Protocol

Role: Staff Software Engineer / Tech Lead expertise, without additional authority. Trigger: a planning request, material uncertainty, architecture or a complex change. Output: a scoped plan tied to verifiable outcomes. Rules: AGHR-009/011/013/034.

Preconditions

Identify the accepted goal, non-goals, current grant and existing architecture. State assumptions. Clarify ambiguity that changes safety, correctness, material scope or authority; document reversible noncritical assumptions and proceed. A plan-only request remains read-only.

Sequence

  1. Observe date/time from a trusted host clock when available, including timezone. If unavailable, disclose it; do not make shell success a prerequisite for all reasoning. Verify current official versions/support only when selection or compatibility depends on them. Choose a supported compatible version and record why; do not blindly upgrade to latest stable.
  2. Model the actual GUI/user or API/data journey as testable goals, including rejection/failure and relevant operational behavior. No feature creep or speculative flexibility.
  3. Choose the simplest complete architecture consistent with existing approved boundaries. Shared/Core, feature grouping, DRY or new abstractions require real semantic value. No arbitrary file-size or line-count optimization.
  4. Design proportionate safe diagnostics and, where needed, durable audit. Specify redaction, destination, buffering/backpressure, failure semantics and measurable budgets; asynchronous is not automatically safe or free.
  5. Record milestones as integrated outcomes, dependencies, tests, candidate/environment needs and risks. Identify existing work to reuse, not merely a list of files to create.
  6. Initialize canonical state and generate PROJECT_MAP sections TECH_STACK, SYSTEM_FLOW, ARCHITECTURE and ORPHANS & PENDING. Keep pending history. The map is a view, not source authority.

Exit

Deliver plan, assumptions, verifiable milestones, proposed changes, needed decisions and next action. Wait when the task is planning-only or a required decision is absent; otherwise an existing implementation mandate can continue without repeated approval. Use templates/PLAN.md and prompts/PLAN.md.

Lifecycle, alternatives and baseline (1.1.0)

Use lifecycle tailoring and smart clarification. Recommend a defensible better option when it matters, without silently changing scope. Record plan baseline, criteria and dependencies in existing project records. Use standards applicability only for the relevant context. The named external methodologies in the optional catalog are not dependencies or governing authorities.

protocols/EXECUTION.md

Execution Engine

Role: technical delivery coordinator. Trigger: authorized implementation against known outcomes. Output: verified scoped product work with honest evidence. Rules: AGHR-009/010/014–028/031/040.

Run contract

Bind goal, acceptance criteria, non-goals, current candidate, environment, provider/host, active grant, resource budgets and pending effects. Reconcile current repository and reuse before writing. 'Full execution' means the documented grant, not unlimited permission.

Working loop

  1. Choose a ready authorized outcome slice. Define its success and relevant negative paths before coding.
  2. Implement complete behavior for that scope, handling errors and necessary observability. No placeholder presented as complete; no unrelated refactoring. Clean only verified new orphans caused by the change.
  3. Verify actual behavior with focused checks, broadening for impact and mandatory gates. Simulation is acceptable only for claims it actually supports. Do not replace required real integration evidence with mocks. Do not assert invisible universal no-regression.
  4. Inspect the diff and record candidate-specific checks, limits and pending work in canonical state. Refresh the map at meaningful transitions and before dependent work/handoff.
  5. Continue the next independent authorized slice. A blocked dependency prevents dependent work, not everything else. Diagnose failure before retry; reconcile partial effects and revalidate approval/policy after waits.

Stop conditions

Owner cancellation; revoked/expired authority; unresolved consequential conflict; missing mandatory prevention; exhausted budget; unsafe or indeterminate effects; or no safe runnable work. Persist checkpoint, blocker owner/resume condition and the next authorized action. Do not loop forever or bypass a guard to satisfy 'continue'.

Completion

Accepted outcome inventory and required checks determine scoped completion. An empty ORPHANS & PENDING section or all tasks marked closed is insufficient. Preserve approved deferred work/limitations explicitly. Implementation verification, independent review, release acceptance, publication and activation remain different states and permissions.

Copyable request: prompts/EXECUTE.md.

Continuous mode and visible delivery (1.1.0)

Offer guided or continuous-within-grant mode once when relevant. A valid implementation grant permits routine in-scope work without repeated permission; mode selection never permits protected effects. Use execution/Git, communication and progress/drift. Material unanswered questions stop dependent effects only. Owner pause stops all scoped work, including independent slices.

Before closeout, disclose the current branch and observed unmerged work, resolve readiness and permission separately, and prepare the next copyable request only when a new session is genuinely useful. No autonomous merge, push, publication or deployment is implied.

protocols/SURGICAL_EDITING.md

Surgical Editing Protocol

Role: Staff Software Engineer. Trigger: a targeted authorized correction or feature change. Output: the smallest coherent change that satisfies the accepted contract.

  1. Impact analysis. Read the relevant current state/map and verify against actual code. Locate affected callers, tests, interfaces, data/schema and deployment boundaries. Check official versioned sources only where API or compatibility uncertainty requires it. State material assumptions.
  2. Preserve and implement. Keep unrelated files, formatting, comments, staged/untracked changes and previous debt intact. Follow valid local patterns; do not preserve an unsafe pattern blindly. Update documentation your change invalidates. Reuse Shared/Core only where semantics warrant it, and add logging only where useful or required.
  3. Prove correction. For a defect, observe a valid failing reproduction when practical and safe, then observe the fix and affected regression checks. If this is impossible, record why and the strongest safe alternative. TDD is a technique, not an excuse to invent a red run or block an urgent authorized mitigation indefinitely.
  4. Review and sync. Check the final diff for unintended edits, secrets, changed assertions and generated files. Remove only confirmed new orphans. Record remaining deprecation/migration work, evidence and candidate identity. Update canonical state and regenerate the map.
  5. Continue or hand off. Continue inside the existing grant. A review-only request never authorizes edits. No automatic commit, push, merge or deployment. Deliver the change, actual checks, gaps and next authorized step.

Copyable request: prompts/EDIT.md. Related rules: AGHR-011–017, AGHR-025–026, AGHR-034.

procedures/REQUEST_INTAKE.md

Unified request and change intake

Applies to: features, fixes, reviews, plans, rules, recommendations, notes, policies, exceptions, removals and source updates. Records: templates/REQUEST.json or the short Markdown form; one canonical project registry.

Short route

For routine work already within a valid grant, record original request/locator, goal, scope/exclusions, criterion, authority reference, disposition and evidence/result. Use templates/SHORT_REQUEST.md. A short request still distinguishes review from implementation and does not skip protected checks.

Full route

  1. Capture the exact available source in approved custody. Record source ID, locator/version, captured time and hash where appropriate. Sensitive sources may use protected references; do not duplicate secrets.
  2. Split compound items without losing conditions, exclusions or original wording. Classify kind, force, institutional authority, host-channel observation, trust, approval, applicability, impact and waiverability.
  3. Identify existing coverage, implementation and conflicts. Determine whether the request is a rule, a product requirement, a task, or information only. Classifying a recommendation does not approve it.
  4. Propose ADD, MERGE, STRENGTHEN, REPLACE, LINK, REJECT, DEFER or NEEDS_INFORMATION. State added value and affected contracts/files/criteria. Avoid synonymous duplicate rules.
  5. Resolve with the proper authority. Existing authorization may permit routine actions. New protected permissions, governance changes, external obligations and unresolved meaningful conflicts need the competent decision. Do not halt independent authorized items.
  6. Apply approved changes through a work item; define acceptance before implementation. Record rule/version and effective-date changes separately from deployment.
  7. Verify the actual candidate and update evidence/state/map. Preserve rejection/deferral history and source relationships. Review is not independent unless genuinely established.

Disposition example

Input: 'Never open any lockfile.' Kind: proposed constraint. Force: proposed MUST_NOT, not effective. Decision: REPLACE with targeted inspection when needed for dependency correctness; avoid unnecessary full-file context loading. Rationale: an absolute read ban would block legitimate security and compatibility review. Target: AGHR-006/013 and ENGINEERING policy. No model parameter or confidence percentage changes its authority.

Unresolved source

Input: '[cite: 1]' without a resolvable source. Preserve it in the original and mark source support UNVERIFIED. Do not invent a publication. A separate reliable reference can support a new proposal but cannot be claimed as the missing original.

CLI

python runtime/cli.py intake --project ../my-project --request request.json validates and saves the request under its unique ID. It never approves, executes or rewrites source policies. Existing IDs are refused, preserving prior records.

Smart clarification (1.1.0)

Apply AGHR-041 before dependent effects: inspect authorized sources, then ask only unresolved material questions with options, impact and a recommendation. Preserve prior valid answers; record a bounded assumption only for reversible noncritical detail. Separate missing source access from missing user intent. Do not treat silence as permission. Continue unaffected authorized work.

Use the detailed procedure. Optional clarifications in REQUEST records capture decisions; the CLI validates records, not the truth of user consent. See record mapping.

verification/LIMITATIONS.md

Assurance limits and residual qualification — 1.1.0

This is the integrated complete reference, not an automatic deployment, vendor endorsement, certified management system or grant of authority. Original architecture and rule identities are preserved; content and executable reference helpers have been updated. The original1.0 narrative and results remain separately labeled historical, not current verification.

The source register has56 records:30 inherited developer/host records plus26 deduplicated new references.29 relevant official pages were opened for selected guidance, metadata and scope during this merge; the other inherited pages were not all independently re-audited. Full licensed ISO texts and complete humanitarian handbooks were not reviewed in this merge. The16 cards and80 recommendations are a bounded reference synthesis, not a full normative-control list, a conformity claim or certification. Humanitarian guidance applies only when context warrants it.

The six provider and six host routes remain documentation-only. The36 original and24 added model/host scenarios are NOT_RUN: no native Codex/Claude/Gemini/OpenCode/Copilot execution, real credentials, model response, production migration or external approval was tested. No independence or release acceptance is granted. Comparing record booleans does not demonstrate intelligent clarification; asking the right natural-language question and safely using real tools require behavioral testing on the selected host.

The bundled Python executor mediates only three local file operations under its trusted-caller/private-workspace assumptions. It does not mediate arbitrary shell, network, MCP or Git actions; eliminate hostile operating-system races; authenticate people; or persist replay protection and audit custody across restarts. Operating-system isolation, all-route mediation, durable authenticated logs and service authorization remain adopter responsibilities. The new progress command is read-only and does not expand that executor.

REQUEST/STATE extension checks validate declared structure, references and artifact bytes. They do not prove that a decision issuer is competent or that a test producer is honest. Clarification records are not wired as an automatic execution gate. The host must bind decisions, current policy identity, grants and effects. Full measurement scope is caller-declared; zero data is unknown, not100% complete. Progress never reports overall compliance or grants release acceptance. Missing Git/remote observations remain unknown. Historical evidence conflicts need explicit reconciliation; do not delete inconvenient failures or accept a forged waiver.

Version1.1 readers accept legacy unextended records. Unmodified1.0 readers are not promised to accept the optional fields. An existing .aghr must undergo a reviewed migration; it must not be overwritten with init. External canonical-tracker integration requires a qualified adapter; no vendor tracker connector is implemented. Optional skills are outside auto-discovery and uninstalled; copying a SKILL.md without resolving its references is not a qualified installation.

Local runtime/tests target Python3.10+ with standard-library dependencies only. Actual execution was Python3.13.5 on Linux. Python3.10 grammar parsing is not execution under that version. Windows/macOS and other interpreters were not tested. The optional HTML authoring tool uses Mistune and BeautifulSoup; these are not runtime dependencies and are not installed automatically. The resulting HTML uses no external scripts/fonts. Desktop/mobile Chromium samples are not a full browser/accessibility certification.

Before use in an actual project: establish real facts and source authority, obtain the applicable adoption decision, qualify model/host loading and protected routes, and bind evidence to that project. Product acceptance, independent review, Git merge/push, publication and activation retain separate authority. None was performed on a user project in this merge.

compatibility/MERGE_REVIEW_AR.md

مراجعة الدمج — AGHR 1.1.0

الأصل والتفويض

الأصل هو الأرشيف الكامل Agent_Governance_Harness_Reference_1.0.0(1).zip المرفق من المالك، وليس AGHR Minimal. جرد الأصل يضم 135 ملفًا، وجرد الإضافات 78 ملفًا. حُفظت هويات المدخلات وبصماتها في سجل المدخلات. التفويض هو مراجعة الأرشيف ودمج الإضافات المتفق عليها في نسخة كاملة؛ لا يشمل تبنيها في مشروع قائم أو تغيير إعدادات مضيف أو دمج Git أو دفعه أو النشر الإنتاجي.

ما حُفظ

حُفظت العقود الأربعة: الحوكمة والتشغيل والإنفاذ والأدلة. بقي registry/RULES.json سجل القواعد الوحيد، وبقيت متطلبات المنتج وقرارات المالك منفصلة عن الأدوات. حُفظت معرفات القواعد الأربعين السابقة. لم تُعدّل بايتات design/frozen/ ولا ملفات compatibility/V4_* التاريخية. هذه السجلات توثّق مراجعة 1.0؛ لا يعني حفظها أن المراجعة التاريخية أعيد تنفيذها باستقلال في هذه الجلسة.

قرارات المصالحة

نُقحت AGHR-010 و011 و023 و038 داخل مواضعها لتغطية الاستمرار والاستيضاح وتأكيد الأثر والمهارات الخارجية. أضيفت AGHR-041 إلى047 لما يحتاج قاعدة مستقلة: الاستيضاح الذكي، البدائل، دورة الحياة، إغلاق الفروع، التواصل، قياس الانحراف، وانطباق المعايير. رُبطت بنود الإضافات الاثنا عشر بالسجل ولم يُنسخ سجل EXT كسلطة ثانية. راجع مصفوفة البنود.

لم تُرقَّ التوصيات الأكاديمية أو بطاقات المعايير أو المهارات الاختيارية إلى قواعد مطبقة على كل مشروع. تؤخذ الوثائق الرسمية المتوافقة مع إصدار النموذج والمضيف مرجعًا تقنيًا؛ لا يمنح أي مرجع خارجي تفويضًا للكتابة أو يتجاوز المتطلبات المعتمدة.

الإدماج الأكاديمي والتشغيلي

دُمجت موضوعات الدليل الإضافي الثمانية عشر في الدليل القائم الذي أصبح 38 بابًا، مع ملف HTML موحد مشتق منه. توجد سبعة إجراءات عربية مفصلة تستدعى عند الصلة، و16 بطاقة معيار و80 توصية ضبط هندسية مصاغة للحزمة، لا نقلًا معياريًا مرخصًا. أضيفت عشر مهارات أصلية اختيارية خارج مسارات الاكتشاف التلقائي. لا يلزم BMAD أو Spec Kit أو Addy أو أية إضافة خارجية لتشغيل أدوات الحزمة.

البيانات والتوافق

أضيف حقلا clarifications وdelivery اختياريًا إلى REQUEST وSTATE، دون إنشاء عقد خامس أو سجل حالة موازٍ. يقرأ الإصدار 1.1 السجلات القديمة غير الممتدة. لا تُفترض قدرة قارئ1.0 على قراءة الحقول الجديدة؛ يلزم اتباع إجراء الترحيل واختبار المضيف والقارئ. المرجع المسجل لقرار أو منح صلاحية ليس إثبات هوية أو تصريحًا تنفذه الأداة تلقائيًا.

أضيف أمر progress للقراءة فقط، يفصل عدد المهام عن معايير القبول، ويعرض نقص الرؤية والتعارض والنتائج الحرجة. لا يستعلم Git أو الخدمات الخارجية ولا يحسب نسبة امتثال كلية. فحص جاهزية السجل يمنع تعارض أدلة النجاح والفشل دون تسوية.

التصرف في اختبارات الإضافات

أُبقيت اختبارات الأصل الـ127، ونُقلت40 حالة اختبار من أداتي القرار والحساب. استُبدلت اختبارات هيكل الإضافات المنفصلة الثمانية باختبارات تكامل جديدة؛ كانت تتضمن شرط عدم توفر الأصل، الذي لم يعد صحيحًا بعد إرفاقه. بقيت نتائج خطي الأساس أدلة تاريخية مستقلة. لم تُستبدل اختبارات فاشلة بمعايير أضعف.

حدود الادعاء

الدمج إنشاء مرجع كامل قابل للمراجعة، لا اعتماد المشروع أو النموذج أو المضيف. التحقق البرمجي يثبت السجلات والروابط وحسابات محددة ومسار التنفيذ المحلي الاصطناعي، وليس فهم اللغة الطبيعية أو منع كل أداة. راجع تقرير التحقق وحدود الاعتماد.

templates/DELIVERY_RECORD_MAPPING.md

Delivery record mapping — one canonical backend

Need Canonical owner Representation
Material question, options, recommendation and decision REQUEST Optional clarifications list; existing decision references
Guided/continuous selection, phase, approved plan baseline STATE Optional delivery object; selection is NOT a grant
Current branch and remote freshness STATE delivery.git_observation; observer supplies facts; CLI does not inspect Git
Deviations and remediation STATE delivery.findings; evidence/check and decision references
Actual authority and protected effects Existing host/operator grant and PROJECT adoption Existing operating contract; no boolean in a template grants permission
Standards applicability Existing decision/pending register templates/STANDARDS_APPLICABILITY.md, referenced by a decision
Optional capability adoption Existing request/decision and host qualification templates/CAPABILITY_REVIEW.md

Old REQUEST/STATE records without extension fields remain valid. Existing external systems can map these fields instead of replacing their canonical database. Request capture validates records but does not authenticate a decision or approve an action. delivery data is not a security policy. Unknown facts remain null/UNKNOWN and must not be converted into safe defaults.
